## Top Fantasy Football Waiver Wire Pickups for Winning Your League
In fantasy football, each manager will draft a team consisting of NFL players. Typically, most rosters consist of one quarterback (QB), two running backs (RB), two wide receivers (WR), one tight end (TE), a kicker (K), a defense (DFST), and several bench spots.
While you may be thinking your team looks complete following the draft, after the opening week of the season, you may have several of your top players suffer an injury. This is where the waiver wire comes into play.
The waiver wire will allow you to address your positions of need throughout the long and enduring season. For example, last season in standard formats, fantasy managers likely picked up Bucky Irving, Tank Bigsby, and even Justin Herbert or Bo Nix early off the waiver wire, and each became a strong option throughout the season.
By using this fantasy football waiver wire tool. You can make the correct and informed decisions to position your team better to finish at the top of the standings at the end of the fantasy football season.  

## Understanding the Waiver Wire Process
The waiver wire consists of players who are currently non-rostered. This means that they were either not selected during your draft or your league has dropped them through the course of the season.
On most fantasy platforms, the players on the wire are ranked by their projected point total for the upcoming week or their total score throughout the season.
In addition, the majority of leagues will likely use a waiver wire priority system that will allow the teams that have the lowest record (or who last the previous week) to have “first bid” on the available players. However, some formats utilize a traditional bidding system using FAAB (free agent acquisition budget), which allows each league manager to place a bid on a given player. 
The team that placed the highest bid will then reward that player. This system is typically used in more competitive settings to prevent the worst team from always getting the best players.
FAAB also incorporates strategic planning, as each team is only given a specific budget at the start of the regular season.
By understanding how your specific league’s waiver wire operates, you will be in a great position to make the ideal decision to win your fantasy football league.  

## Key Features of a Fantasy Waiver Wire Tool
This waiver wire tool allows you to compare numerous players at different positions to determine which one you should prioritize on the [waiver wire](https://www.rotoballer.com/category/nfl/fantasy-football-advice-analysis/waiver-wire-articles). When you select specific players, the tool highlights which player ranks higher. This advice is quite clear to digest.
This waiver wire tool will also include their respective positional ranking, which also provides users with a different perspective on their potential long-term value. This comparison contains up-to-date [rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860) and projections that will take injuries and projected snap shares into account, which is a valuable guide to follow.
On this tool, the players are identified by their specific team, such as (ATL, DEN, MIA, CAR), and position (QB, RB, WR, TE).
This waiver wire tool also contains easy-to-use features that can help the user find which players they are looking to compare very quickly.
Lastly, this tool is entirely free to use and applies to all league types and formats. Using our lineup sync function, you can import your roster for easier access.  

## Top Waiver Wire Pickups
Knowing which players to pick up can play a significant role in pushing your team up in your league standings. Throughout the season, the waiver wire often features many emerging players, making it difficult to decide which one to add.
Last season, fantasy managers saw Jerome Ford start the season on a high note, and wide receiver Nick Westbrook-Ikhine flash immense TD upside during a month-long stretch. However, by using these player rankings, fantasy managers can easily determine which player has the highest rest-of-season outlook and which player's production could be unsustainable.
Throughout the season, many depth chart changes can also shake up the wire. When Russell Wilson took over the starting job in the Steel City from Justin Fields, he was likely a highly coveted pick on many waiver wires. However, this tool would have advised you to slightly pump the brakes and avoid overspending your FAAB budget on the aging QB.
In addition, it is also crucial to take a closer look at some lesser-known players, such as potential handcuff running backs like Jordan Mason, Patrick Taylor Jr., and Trey Benson. When Mason earned a starting job, he was viewed as a must-start player in all formats. By using this tool, you will not miss out on any of the top players during the year and have your roster deep and covered at all positions.  

## Using the Football Waiver Wire Rankings to Your Advantage
In order to win your fantasy football league, you must pay vital attention to the waiver wire at all times. By doing this, you will always be one step ahead of your league mates and will continue to roster the most opportunistic players.
Another way to gain an edge is to monitor who your fellow league mates drop. These players then proceed and eventually hit the waiver wire. If your teammates were impatient and dropped a veteran who was having a slow start, like Aaron Rodgers, you could have secured a solid QB1 by the end of the season. Last season, Rodgers had a modest start but found his footing in the second half when Davante Adams was eventually traded to the Jets.
Fantasy managers should also pay close attention to their weekly matchups when adding players. Targeting the right kicker and/or defense can push you over the edge in a tight weekly matchup. Most of the top streaming defenses can likely be found on your waiver wire.  

## How to Use the Fantasy Football Waiver Wire
The first step is to identify the positions your team needs. If you are lacking at quarterback (QB), running back (RB), wide receiver (WR), and/or tight end (TE), prioritize that position at the top of your waiver claim list.
If you're falling behind in the running back depth, it is not a bad idea to prioritize an emerging rookie who may have higher upside during the season's stretch run. In 2024, several rookies like Bucky Irving and Jalen McMillan reached the highest value peaks during the fantasy football playoffs, but both were likely on your waiver wire over the first month of the season.
By keeping a close eye on who is available and, more importantly, who is dropped, you will always stay ahead of your league mates and will be able to make the necessary adjustments to push your team over the edge.  

## Determining How Many Players to Compare
This fantasy football waiver wire tool allows you to choose up to six players at a given time. While this may seem overwhelming at first, the tool provides clear-to-read, digestible data targeted to your league.
The players near the top are typically the popular searches.
However, while you can compare numerous players at once, it is wise only to compare QBs to other QBs and not to flex position and vice versa. Flex positions like RB, WR, and TE can carry different value than the QB position.
If your team needs a QB, it's more important to prioritize the position over the top-ranked TE on the waiver wire.
## Staying Ahead of the Competition
The final component of mastering the waiver wire is staying ahead of your competitors. While the tool helps you identify the top-ranked player, understanding recent news can give you an even further advantage.
For example, if a veteran running back is viewed as the top-ranked player but has begun to receive fewer opportunities, it may be wise also to stash their backup in case the starter falls out of favor with the coaching staff.
While this may seem like a bold move, the managers who opted to stash Tank Bigsby over the heavily-favored Travis Etienne Jr. likely reaped the benefits throughout most of the 2024 campaign.  

## Use Our Waiver Wire Tool to Win Your Fantasy League
To win your fantasy league, you need to make strategic moves on the waiver wire to add the necessary depth and upside to your roster. While you cannot predict injuries, being active on the waiver wire can put you in the best position to succeed despite the “luck” and "hope" aspects of fantasy football. It will take some work, but this tool contains vital information to assist you.
Dedicated fantasy managers typically monitor the waiver wire in their league numerous times each week and generally are the ones who target the correct players.
With a focused approach, you'll put yourself in the best possible position to win your fantasy football league and bring home the championship.

# NFL Survivor Pool Picks Tool - Survivor Planner and Grid
## NFL Survivor Pool Picks Tool
RotoBaller's free **NFL Survivor Pool Picks Tool** helps you make the best pick every week. Plan and optimize your picks for the entire season. Click your preferred matchup in the grid for each week, sort by Vegas odds, filter out risky matchups, and plan out your picks for the whole NFL schedule. You can create and save as many entries as you want, entirely for free. Read more on [how to master NFL Survivor Pool strategy](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-survivor-pool-strategy-expert-tips-for-survivor-leagues/1519975).
To save an entry in the Survivor Tool, make sure you are logged in, then name your entry and click at least one matchup inside the grid (in one of the "week" columns). [See below](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-survivor-grid-football-survivor-pool-picks-tool#About) for many more details on how to use the tool.

Methodology every number, explained 

Clean View
    Hides every team you have not picked. A finished plan uses 18 of 32 teams spread down the grid, so this collapses it to just those rows and lets you read the whole season across the week columns at once. Your picks are untouched — it only changes what is on screen — and it switches itself off visually when there is nothing picked yet. 

Strikes & Strikes End
    A strike pool lets you lose without going out, so those weeks are not worth protecting the way a sudden-death week is. Tell the tool how many strikes you have and the last week they apply, and the optimizer treats losses in that window as survivable: it will deliberately spend weak teams early and hold the strong ones for the weeks that actually eliminate you. Four strikes through Week 4 makes that window effectively free, which is why the plan can open with teams it would never otherwise touch. 

Avoid After Bye-Week
    Skips a team in the week straight after its bye, using the same penalty as the other avoid rules. Which way this cuts is a genuine judgement call — a rested team can be the healthier one, or the rustier one — so it is off by default and there for pools where you have a view on it. 

Avoid Chalk
    Skips the most popular team each week: this week that is the highest Yahoo! pick share, and in the weeks ahead - where no pick data exists yet - the biggest favourite. The penalty is heavier than the other avoid rules (about a quarter of the team's odds) because the point is to be off the crowd, so the chalk is only taken when every alternative is clearly worse. In a big pool that is how you gain on the field; in a small one it mostly costs win probability. 

Avoid Divisional / Away Games
    Optimizer rules, separate from the grid's display toggles. Divisional opponents know each other well and upset more often; road favourites win less than the same team at home. Turning either on docks those games about 8% of their odds - a tiebreaker, so the plan routes around them when it costs little and still takes a clearly better team when it does not. The odds already price in the matchup, so a heavier penalty double-counted it and gave up far more survival than it saved. 

EV column
    Survivor expected value for the selected week: a team's win odds ÷ the field's average survival odds (each team's win odds weighted by its share of picks). Over 1.00, surviving the week grows your share of the pool; under 1.00 the crowd grows faster. 

Pick popularity
    Two real sources, never modeled: RotoBaller users' saved entries (used once 50+ entries exist) and Yahoo Survival Football's public pick distribution, refreshed every 6 hours, blended when both exist. 

Pool Size & Strikes
    These steer the optimizer. In a small pool surviving is winning, so it simply takes the best odds. In a big pool the whole field lands on the same favourite and surviving alongside them gains you nothing, so the plan charges a penalty for a team's share of the picks and will trade some win probability to get off the chalk. Strikes push the same way: a loss you can absorb makes a contrarian pick cheaper. The penalty scales with pool size (and strikes) up to a ceiling, so a heavily-backed favourite is never dropped for a genuine long shot — and only the week being played is affected, because pick popularity is not published for future weeks. 

Optimize
    An exact assignment solve (not a greedy pick-the-favorite): each remaining week gets a distinct team, maximizing the product of win odds - the mathematical probability of surviving to week 18. It is deterministic: the same win probabilities, used teams and manual picks always produce the same path; it changes only when the data refreshes (multiple times daily) or your entry changes. Your manual picks are always kept; Remove takes back only the optimizer's picks. 

Win probability (W%)
    RotoBaller's model, built from Vegas lines plus efficiency stats, refreshed multiple times a day. Where the model hasn't published a number, the optimizer falls back to the live moneyline.
## NFL Survivor Pool Picks Tool - Make the Optimal Survivor Picks for the Entire NFL Season
RotoBaller's **Survivor Pool Picks Tool** allows you to plan and optimize your survivor pool picks every week for the whole season. Here are some tips for how to best use this tool:
  * **NFL Schedule** - See the entire NFL schedule in an easy-to-read grid
  * **Unlimited Entries** - Create as many entries as you want for free! Save an entry by naming it in the 'Create an Entry' field, and making one selection in the grid by clicking on the matchup. Make as many selections for as many weeks as you want! If you make additional selections or changes, you can update your first entry or create new entries.
  * **Track Used Teams** - If you create a new entry in Week 7, you can mark all your previously used teams off in the 'Previously Used Teams' field, rather than actually making selections on the grid for Week 1-6. Then "hide" the used teams with the toggle to declutter the grid.
  * **Vegas Odds** - Switch between money lines and spreads from DraftKings. Notice the green highlights update to indicate favorable spreads or money lines.
  * **Pick %** - Our users are experienced Survivor pool players. We surface Pick % data for every week from within the RotoBaller's Survivor Pool Picks Tool, rather than relying on sites like Yahoo.
  * **Win %** - Sort matchups by RotoBaller's proprietary Win Probability, calculated using Vegas odds and advanced stats like DVOA.
  * **Future Value** - The Future Value column shows how much value a team has beyond the current week. The average is 50, so at 0 has no future value, while a team at 100 is the top team for Future Value. Our calculation uses RotoBaller's proprietary Win probability for all games a team is favored.
  * **Stats** - This column shows three helpful stats: W/L record, Points for and against, and a team's streak.
  * **Filter Bad Games** - Use toggles to filter out primetime matchups like MNF or TNF, tricky road matchups in away games, or divisional opponents.
  * **Double Picks** - If your pool has double picks, use this field to indicate the week they start.
  * **Daily Updates** - All data in the tool (Odds, Win %, Pick %, Future Value, Stats) is updated multiple times throughout the day, as injuries and other depth chart shakeups happen.
  * Contact us if you have any issues. 


## **What is an NFL Survivor Pool?**
An NFL survivor pool is a contest that requires users to pick a different NFL team to win every week. Instead of picking each game like a traditional pick 'em pool, participants pick one team to win per week until the start of the NFL playoffs. While it's a tough season-long grind, it's a fun way to compete throughout the entire season.
When making your weekly picks, weighing factors and data such as pick popularity, win probability, point spreads, and future matchups is key. If your pick loses, you are eliminated from the competition and lose the chance to share some of the prize pool.
Saving popular teams for later in the season is just one of many weekly strategies for success, and the winners of Survivor Pools often utilize a data-driven approach.
There are large-scale NFL survivor pools with big prizes, but you can also play with your friends, and pool sizes vary widely. Your pool's specific rules can vary, too, but the majority of survivor contests are typically uniform.
## **What is the NFL Survivor Pool Picks Tool?**
The **NFL Survivor Pool Picks Tool** helps users plan and identify the optimal picks for a survivor pool by combining future matchups, expected value, and popular picks percentage to create a survivor strategy for an entire NFL season.
This tool is designed to give you the best chance to win and stay on track by providing the best picks of the week based on future picks and rest-of-season outlooks.
## **NFL Survivor Tips and Strategies**
  * **Pick Sneaky Favorites Early** : choose mid-level or under-the-radar teams playing at home and against weaker opponents early in the season to save Tier 1 teams for later in the year.
  * **Plan Ahead:** With the assistance of RotoBaller’s NFL Survivor League tool, map out your future picks to avoid burning top teams early. Identify when bye weeks could impact your future picks and create a season-long outline for your weekly picks.
  * **Fade Popular Picks:** Target undervalued NFL teams with plus matchups, but avoid the most popular teams to achieve weekly survivor differentiation. This game theory is one of the more impactful strategic mechanisms to employ throughout the season.
  * **Be Wary of Divisional Games:** While you don't necessarily have to avoid all NFL divisional matchups, familiarity with an opponent can tighten the gap between two teams, leading to more upsets in divisional games, especially on the road.
  * **Save Safe Playoff Teams for Later:** While elite or high-end teams are favored throughout much of the season, saving them for later when playoff incentives are on the line can give you a sneaky edge
  * **Avoid Small Road Favorites:** Mitigate risk by steering away from small road favorites, especially if a favored team traveled to the opposite coast for a cross-country matchup.
  * **Examine Game Results Closely:** Picking a team with a poor record but close point differentials is a sneaky strategy, while picking a team with a good record but close point differentials could be a trap
  * **NFL Odds, Money Lines, and Game Totals:** Review the weekly NFL odds, money lines, and game totals to get a strong idea of who the sharps feel are the favorites, and by how much they expect the margin of victory to be. Generally speaking, the larger the point spread, the less chance of an upset occurring.


## NFL Survivor FAQs
  * **How do NFL survivor pools work?**


In NFL survivor pools, players must pick one team to win each week. However, players cannot pick the same team twice, so selections should be considered carefully. If the team you pick loses, you are eliminated from the competition. The goal is to make it all 18 weeks with correct picks. All participants who make it to the end will share the final prize pool.  

  * **Can I pick the same team twice?**


No. In most survivor pools, you cannot pick the same team twice in a season. Once you select a team for a given week, that team is no longer available for a future pick. This factor should lead participants to map out future picks and save strong teams for later in the season.  

  * **What happens if a game is canceled?**


Depending on your league setup, if you pick a team from a canceled game, you could be eliminated from the competition, while other leagues will have more lenient rules that allow you to survive to the next week.  

  * **Is it better to fade/avoid the most popular picks?**


Yes. If you pick the same team as many others, you run the risk of spilling the final prize or losing a tie-breaker. By differentiating your picks, you have a better chance at winning the top spot. If you make a risky pick and it pays off, you have the chance to survive, while many of the other participants might be eliminated.

#  NFL Pick 'Em Pool Picks (Week 7) - Targets, Avoids, Predictions for Pick'em Contests (2026) 
[ See RotoBaller at the top of Google ](https://google.com/preferences/source?q=rotoballer.com) by [Andersen Pickard](https://www.rotoballer.com/author/pickandersen "Posts by Andersen Pickard")
Link copied to clipboard!
Reddit Twitter Facebook Copy link
[ @AndersenPickard ](https://twitter.com/AndersenPickard)
![Jordan Love - Fantasy Football Rankings, NFL Injury News, DFS Lineup Picks](https://www.rotoballer.com/wp-content/uploads/2024/12/Jordan-Love-Fantasy-Football-Rankings-NFL-Injury-News-DFS-Lineup-Picks-icon-rotoballer-1220x813-1220x813.jpeg) Oct 25, 2026, 3:50 AM ET
The top NFL Pick'em pool picks for Week 7 of the 2026 NFL season. Andersen's weekly NFL Pick'em pool targets, avoids, and predictions to help win pick'em contests.
Welcome back to our NFL Pick'em Pool Picks for Week 7 of the 2026 NFL season. The midway point of the season will be here before you know it, which is crazy to think about! As we get into Week 7, we're able to determine which teams are the real deal and which ones can't be trusted.
Some questions do still exist, though. Is [Drake Maye](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/24958/Drake+Maye) the MVP favorite? Will Miami ever get a win? Is it time for Rams fans to panic?
Every week of the 2026 NFL season, I'll lock in picks for each game and tier them based on my confidence level for the selection. The games will be grouped into locks, semi-confident picks, and toss-ups. The number listed next to the game shows my confidence level, counting down to my least confident pick. The winner picks are italicized. Let's get to the picks for Week 7.
**_Bookmark our updated and revamped[fantasy football rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860) portal for all league formats!_**   
  
[PPR rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860/rankings?spreadsheet=ppr) | [Half-PPR rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860/rankings?spreadsheet=half-ppr) | [Standard rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860/rankings?spreadsheet=standard) | [Rookie rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860/rankings?spreadsheet=rookie&league=Overall) | [Superflex rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860/rankings?spreadsheet=superflex&league=Overall) | [Dynasty rankings](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-rankings-tiered-ppr/265860/rankings?spreadsheet=dynasty&league=Overall) | [Who To Start?](https://www.rotoballer.com/who-should-i-start-fantasy-football-weekly-matchups) | [Injuries](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-injuries-report-fantasy-football-injury-updates) | [Matchup Ratings](https://www.rotoballer.com/fantasy-football-projections-matchup-ratings) | [Strength of Schedule](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl-fantasy-football-strength-of-schedule)
## Week 7 NFL Pick'em Pool Locks
The number listed next to the game shows my confidence level, counting down to my least-confident pick. The higher the number, the better. The winner picks are italicized.
  * **14: Dolphins at _Jets_**


The Dolphins are coming off their bye week, but even the extra rest won't help them dig out of the basement of the AFC. Miami's offense has fallen to dead last in points scored per game (14.0). The Jets aren't a whole lot better offensively, but their defense has withstood some tougher tests while allowing the seventh-fewest points per game (20.3).
  * **13:_49ers_ at Falcons**


At this point, it would seem crazy to pick against the 49ers, who have cruised to 6-0. Atlanta can learn from Washington, which did seem to slow down the Niners in the second half of Week 6. However, at the end of the day, San Francisco will just be too strong on paper, led by potential MVP quarterback [Brock Purdy](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/23241/Brock+Purdy).
  * **12:_Browns_ at Titans**


We never thought we'd see the day that the Browns were a "lock" to win, but here we are. [Deshaun Watson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/18857/Deshaun+Watson) has been playing some of the best football of his career, and Cleveland's defense has held strong with the seventh-fewest points allowed over the previous three weeks. Meanwhile, the Titans are last in offensive yards per game and tied for last in offensive points per game.
  * **11: Colts at _Vikings_**


The Vikings have been a pleasant surprise this year, cruising to 5-0 with an elite defensive system in place. Minnesota has allowed the fewest points per game, and the gap between them and the second-place team is a whopping 3.8 points. The Vikings have also allowed just 194.3 yards per game over the last three weeks, which is the best mark in football during that span.
## Semi-Confident Picks For Week 7 Pick 'Em Pools
  * **10: Giants at _Texans_**


Turmoil continues in New York, especially on the offensive side of the ball. [Jameis Winston](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/16762/Jameis+Winston) has been benched for [J.J. McCarthy](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/24956/J.J.+McCarthy), [Cam Skattebo](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/25887/Cam+Skattebo) found himself deep in the doghouse, and [Najee Harris](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/21768/Najee+Harris) was released after the team rolled out a backfield committee approach. With the Giants in shambles, we're taking the Texans.
  * **9:_Rams_ at Raiders**


There are some serious question marks surrounding this Rams team, and nothing is a given for them anymore. With that said, we feel decent about their odds against the Raiders, who have allowed 36 points per game over the last three weeks (most in the NFL).
  * **8:_Chiefs_ at Seahawks**


After missing the playoffs last year, the Chiefs are back with a vengeance this season. Seattle is not an easy team to beat, especially in Seattle. With that being said, Kansas City has the most offensive yards per game and the fifth-most points scored per game. The Chiefs offense against the Seahawks defense will be one of the most exciting matchups to watch this week.
  * **7:_Cowboys_ at Eagles**


The Eagles are in absolute disarray right now due to injuries on both sides of the ball. The running back room has been hit the hardest, but perhaps no injury is worse than quarterback [Jalen Hurts](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/21831/Jalen+Hurts) dealing with blood clots. [Tanner McKee](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/24124/Tanner+McKee) will be the starting quarterback for the rest of the season, and he'll have trouble keeping pace with a Dallas offense that ranks third in total offense.
##  **NFL Pick 'Em****Toss-Ups Of Week 7**
  * **6: Buccaneers at _Panthers_**


It's still unclear whether [Baker Mayfield](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/19790/Baker+Mayfield) (thumb) will return to action this week. If he plays, this game will stay in the toss-up section. However, if it's another week of [Jalon Daniels](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/25877/Jalon+Daniels), we'd feel far more confident about the Panthers. Credit to Daniels, though, for going from undrafted status to keeping the Bucs afloat during Mayfield's absence.
  * **5:_Broncos_ at Cardinals**


The Cardinals are a confusing team to figure out. We'll take the Broncos in this one, but Arizona has proven that it can hang around with just about any team. This final score could be closer than expected, as it seems like the Cardinals have put up a real fight against most of their opponents in 2026. Most recently, they took down the Rams on the road.
  * **4:_Packers_ at Lions**


After a shaky start to the season, Green Bay has turned a corner and is absolutely surging into Week 7. Meanwhile, the Lions are well-rested coming off a bye. This is anyone's game, but we're giving a slight edge to the Packers because of how elite this passing attack has looked lately. The offense will also add another element this week as running back [Josh Jacobs](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/20824/Josh+Jacobs) returns to action.
  * **3:_Patriots_ at Bears**


It has been a tale of two different three-week stretches for Maye, who went from a liability to a potential MVP candidate. He has 11 total touchdowns over his last three games, but the next stretch of his schedule is admittedly trickier, starting with the Bears on Thursday Night Football. We're taking New England, but [Caleb Williams](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/24927/Caleb+Williams) and the Bears will keep this incredibly close.
  * **2:_Bengals_ at Ravens**


Believe it or not, the Ravens and Bengals are third and fourth in the AFC North, respectively. However, both teams are also above .500. This Week 7 battle could have enormous playoff implications, and we're giving a slight edge to the Bengals because of how difficult it is to slow down their passing game. Another wrinkle for Baltimore is the fact that [Lamar Jackson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/19781/Lamar+Jackson) (ankle) may not be ready to play this week.
  * **1:_Steelers_ at Saints**


The Saints held on to win last week, even though the Giants made things very interesting in the fourth quarter. They'll get a tougher test this week against the Steelers, who have been playing well on both sides of the ball. Although people typically think of the Steelers as a defense-first team, Pittsburgh's offense has the seventh-most points per game over the last three weeks.

#  NFL Survivor Pool Picks: Week 7 Targets and Avoids 
[ See RotoBaller at the top of Google ](https://google.com/preferences/source?q=rotoballer.com) by [Kipp Heisterman](https://www.rotoballer.com/author/kipp628 "Posts by Kipp Heisterman")

Reddit Twitter Facebook Copy link
[ @kipppsta ](https://twitter.com/kipppsta)
![](https://www.rotoballer.com/wp-content/uploads/2023/09/C.J.-Stroud-fantasy-football-draft-sleepers-rankings-quarterbacks-NFL-DFS-picks-icon-rotoballer-2-1220x789.jpg) Oct 25, 2026, 3:00 AM ET
The top NFL survivor pool picks for Week 7 of the 2026 NFL season. Kipp's weekly survivor league pool targets and avoids -- expert picks for survivor leagues.
Welcome back to our **NFL Survivor Pool Picks for Week 7** of the 2026 NFL season! For anyone who isn't familiar, each week we give you our recommended Survivor Pool picks to target and also avoid for 2026 Survivor leagues.
This week, I'm pinch-hitting on this feature for our friend Kevin Tompkins -- kind of like the backup quarterback coming off the bench.
Our top pick last week was New England, which handled the New York Jets relatively cleanly and helped us move on if you selected it. Additionally, our second choice was San Francisco, which won on Monday Night Football against Washington. This week, we hope to go 2-for-2 on selections again!

Now, let's look at the Survivor pool pick recommendations to target and avoid for Week 7.
## Top Survivor Pool Picks to Target for Week 7
#### **Houston Texans vs. New York Giants**
The first selection this week is the Houston Texans, who are facing an interconference matchup with the New York Giants. These two teams are headed in opposite directions, as the Texans have won two straight while the Giants have tied and lost in their previous two games.
If you recall, Houston started 0-3 last season before going on an absolute tear to finish the season 12-5, winning in the Wild Card Round before being defeated by the New England Patriots in the Divisional Round. The Texans appear to be on a similar track this year and should be able to handle this Giants squad.
Houston has been markedly improved on offense this season, averaging 24 points per game and 355 yards of total offense, while the Giants have struggled a bit defensively, allowing over 23 points per game and over 135 rushing yards per game.
Houston is also allowing just 83 rushing yards per game on defense, and while it has struggled against the pass, this may be a week it is able to get away with pedestrian numbers going up against a backup quarterback in [J.J. McCarthy](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/24956/J.J.+McCarthy). Houston should be able to handle the Giants at home, and the 9.5-point spread in favor of Houston tells that story as well.
#### **Los Angeles Rams at Las Vegas Raiders**
The Los Angeles Rams did many survivor players a disservice last week by losing to the Arizona Cardinals; however, if you are still reading this piece, that means they likely did you a solid service in eliminating a large portion of your pool.
This week, the Rams travel down the road to take on the Las Vegas Raiders. While the Raiders have been frisky this season, I think the Rams should be able to handle business backed by a very stout defense and a very capable offense.
Overall, the Rams are allowing just 294 yards of total offense to opposing teams, which ranks third-best in the league. They are also racking up over 400 yards of total offense, so their 2-4 record to begin the season feels particularly unlucky.
The Raiders, on the other hand, enter this matchup 3-3 off a loss against the Buffalo Bills, and have struggled quite a bit defensively this season. They are allowing an average of 27 points per game and over 370 yards of total offense per game, both of which rank in the bottom 10 of the league.
While their offense has been productive, scoring 27 points per game, they may find it difficult to get the ball moving through the air against a Rams secondary that is only allowing 174 passing yards per game, which ranks second-best in the league.
The Rams should be able to start righting the ship against the Raiders this week.
#### **Denver Broncos at Arizona Cardinals**
This is another one that I think the favorite in Denver should win, and I am comfortable enough to put it on an entry given what we have seen out of the Broncos lately, as they have defeated the Los Angeles Rams, Jacksonville Jaguars, and Seattle Seahawks in recent weeks. They also have added rest after playing on Thursday Night Football.
Arizona is sitting at 2-4, but it has played in some high-scoring, competitive games, however, their rushing defense has not been exactly up to snuff, which is something I often like to target when making a Survivor pick.
Denver is also allowing an average of nearly 140 rushing yards per game, which is a bit scary, but the Cardinals backfield does not scare me enough to take the Broncos off of my list.
Denver has the better roster and defense, and should win this game.
#### **Also Consider Using**
  * New York Jets (vs. Miami Dolphins)


## **Top Survivor Pool Picks to Avoid for Week 7**
#### Minnesota Vikings vs. Indianapolis Colts
This is a game I think the Vikings should handle, as they are currently sitting at 5-0 and are rocking along with one of the better defenses in the league. But I have a hard time backing them with this offense, and to be fair, I already used Minnesota back in Week 4 against the Miami Dolphins.
As mentioned, the Vikings have a defense that ranks first in the league by allowing under 13 points per game and second in the league by allowing just 260 yards of total offense. That being said, they are scoring just 22 points per game and averaging under 300 yards of total offense.
The Colts defense has been horrible, allowing over 350 yards of total offense per game and over 27 points per game, but it is hard for me to trust Minnesota, even against this defense. The Colts have also shown a capable offense, scoring 24 points per game on average.
Again, I think Minnesota ultimately wins this one, but I could easily have a one-possession game taking place here, and that is a sweat I would be looking to avoid.
#### **Baltimore Ravens vs. Cincinnati Bengals**
I am not sure how many folks are looking to do battle in Survivor with an AFC North rivalry, but we will cover it here as an avoid just in case. The Ravens are three-point home favorites against the Bengals with the expectation that [Lamar Jackson](https://www.rotoballer.com/nfl/player/19781/Lamar+Jackson) will return.
That being said, these are frequently entertaining matchups; however, they are not ideal selections for Survivor. Across the previous six meetings between these two teams, three have been decided by three points or fewer.
#### **Also Consider Fading**
  * Dallas Cowboys (at Philadelphia Eagles)
